NGUYÊN LÝ CỰC TRỊ (EXTREMAL PRINCIPLES)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><table id="359c5e6f-95bd-80e0-8b47-c7b2fde01fd6" class="simple-table"><thead class="simple-table-header"><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8079-b692-ce97e17302eb"><th id="&lt;?EH" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Nguyên lý</th><th id="^}m{" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Công thức</th><th id="nESl" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Hệ thống</th></tr></div></thead><tbody><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8036-99f1-c7d1c4bf4fa0"><td id="&lt;?EH" class="">Tác dụng tối thiểu</td><td id="^}m{" class=""><code>δS = 0</code></td><td id="nESl" class="">Vật lý</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-803e-b57b-f6607e87574f"><td id="&lt;?EH" class="">Năng lượng tối thiểu</td><td id="^}m{" class=""><code>E = min</code></td><td id="nESl" class="">Lượng tử, hóa học</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80e7-aa09-cf020de2f6c2"><td id="&lt;?EH" class="">Entropy cực đại</td><td id="^}m{" class=""><code>H = max</code></td><td id="nESl" class="">Thông tin, 
nhiệt động</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-803c-8864-da7eb7190291"><td id="&lt;?EH" class="">Lợi nhuận tối đa</td><td id="^}m{" class=""><code>E[R] - ½λ·Var(R) = max</code></td><td id="nESl" class="">Dòng tiền</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8028-98ff-dea46f120858"><td id="&lt;?EH" class="">Góc lệch tối thiểu</td><td id="^}m{" class=""><code>θ = min</code></td><td id="nESl" class="">Ánh sáng (Fermat)</td></tr></div></tbody></table></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80ad-98ec-c8518dc66a25"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="359c5e6f-95bd-805c-9e9f-d0a5f2417caf" class="">PHẦN 9: CÁC HẰNG SỐ TỶ LỆ &quot;KHÔNG ĐỔI&quot; XUYÊN HỆ THỐNG</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="359c5e6f-95bd-80df-82f2-ed45e5307d17" class="">27. 
TỶ LỆ 1/2 (0.5) – SÂU HƠN</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-8045-b33c-d4d920246df7" class=""><strong>Các biểu hiện:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><table id="359c5e6f-95bd-80ef-9b6e-d1d65e42f1f2" class="simple-table"><thead class="simple-table-header"><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8082-b90e-e6a53f57653c"><th id="p@b@" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Hệ thống</th><th id="i_DP" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Công thức có ½</th></tr></div></thead><tbody><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-802a-9808-d7946ced0d04"><td id="p@b@" class="">Lượng tử (spin)</td><td id="i_DP" class=""><code>s = ½</code></td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8097-abe7-c564194839ba"><td id="p@b@" class="">Lượng tử (bất định)</td><td id="i_DP" class=""><code>Δx·Δp ≥ ℏ/2</code></td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80eb-9759-e721a45fd236"><td id="p@b@" class="">Thống kê (trung vị)</td><td id="i_DP" class=""><code>P(x &lt; 
median) = 0.5</code></td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80ed-bec7-f0aebbc66241"><td id="p@b@" class="">Tín hiệu (SNR = 0 dB)</td><td id="i_DP" class=""><code>P_signal = P_noise</code></td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-800a-818d-ceadc43824f9"><td id="p@b@" class="">Học (ngưỡng)</td><td id="i_DP" class=""><code>p = 0.5</code> (ngang nhiên)</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80ba-b892-ffb98a9921bb"><td id="p@b@" class="">Di truyền (phân ly)</td><td id="i_DP" class=""><code>P(A) = 0.5</code> (Mendel)</td></tr></div></tbody></table></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8056-86d5-c4025f21df99"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="359c5e6f-95bd-8094-9265-c0d705f4b5b0" class="">28. 
TỶ LỆ 1/e (≈ 0.3679)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-80aa-9122-eb435eeedfc1" class=""><strong>Công thức:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="359c5e6f-95bd-8094-848f-e4f3184b6491" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">1/e = lim_{n→∞} (1 - 1/n)ⁿ</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-802d-9f19-f6cdf54edf62" class=""><strong>Xuất hiện trong:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><table id="359c5e6f-95bd-8038-ab9f-fdcda1fc5b13" class="simple-table"><thead class="simple-table-header"><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8066-a307-c2096cc4f2dc"><th id="fO`u" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Hệ thống</th><th id="TIPF" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Công thức</th></tr></div></thead><tbody><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8085-b62b-dc301ddf5a6e"><td id="fO`u" class="">Phân rã</td><td id="TIPF" class=""><code>N(τ) = N₀/e</code> (thời gian sống)</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8058-ab36-ece1982640ab"><td id="fO`u" class="">Xác suất không sự kiện trong Poisson</td><td id="TIPF" class=""><code>P(0) = e^{-λ}</code></td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-803b-b04e-fb281731819d"><td id="fO`u" class="">Secretary problem</td><td id="TIPF" class=""><code>1/e</code> là ngưỡng tối ưu</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8091-9f20-e179e03cdce3"><td id="fO`u" class="">Nhiệt động</td><td id="TIPF" class=""><code>exp(-E/kT)</code></td></tr></div></tbody></table></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8012-a453-e0f64731a93c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="359c5e6f-95bd-8053-8497-cad425695085" class="">29. 
TỶ LỆ π/4 ≈ 0.7854</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-8016-ba7f-e7a08c60a6aa" class=""><strong>Công thức:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="359c5e6f-95bd-80c2-abf6-d57b51b182ac" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">π/4 = 1 - 1/3 + 1/5 - 1/7 + ... (Leibniz)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-802f-9a35-f26e35981585" class=""><strong>Xuất hiện trong:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="359c5e6f-95bd-80f6-9808-ee527fbeb97e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Xác suất hai số nguyên ngẫu nhiên nguyên tố cùng nhau: <code>6/π²</code> (≈ 0.6079), không phải π/4</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="359c5e6f-95bd-8015-9fa8-cf2b020bdc9f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Hình học: tỷ lệ diện tích hình tròn nội tiếp hình vuông: <code>π/4</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8078-8df0-d215e6e7b71f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="359c5e6f-95bd-801b-ba79-ef6df97dfa08" class="">30. 
TỶ LỆ VÀNG NGHỊCH ĐẢO 1/φ ≈ 0.618</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-800f-a0fd-e3a0e89b1f10" class=""><strong>Công thức:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="359c5e6f-95bd-80b9-a29c-ca66dcfd0894" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">1/φ = φ - 1 = (√5 - 1)/2 ≈ 0.618</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-803e-80e9-f2fdf9dae3b9" class=""><strong>Xuất hiện trong:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="359c5e6f-95bd-80ca-a73e-d09e6bcaeb05" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Hình học (tỷ lệ vàng)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="359c5e6f-95bd-8070-82d3-d9291ea79eb0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Nghệ thuật, kiến trúc</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="359c5e6f-95bd-80a2-8130-db7a162f3399" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">DNA (góc xoắn ~ 137.5° = 360° × 0.382, không phải 0.618)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80c3-93e9-e85414badf43"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="359c5e6f-95bd-80d7-b620-c4f895f7cdb5" class="">PHẦN 10: BẢNG TỔNG HỢP CÁC HẰNG SỐ &quot;DẠNG SỐ&quot; 
XUẤT HIỆN LẶP LẠI</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><table id="359c5e6f-95bd-8002-a54a-e5bab77b49b2" class="simple-table"><thead class="simple-table-header"><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-805e-a342-d74d3b3e8bc1"><th id="eauP" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Tên</th><th id="nDAx" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Giá trị</th><th id="phre" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Dạng</th><th id="{a;&lt;" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Xuất hiện trong (số hệ thống)</th></tr></div></thead><tbody><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8066-a83b-da11379fb015"><td id="eauP" class="">0</td><td id="nDAx" class="">0</td><td id="phre" class="">Số nguyên</td><td id="{a;&lt;" class="">17/17</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8084-9728-e797e999ad52"><td id="eauP" class="">1</td><td id="nDAx" class="">1</td><td id="phre" class="">Số nguyên</td><td id="{a;&lt;" class="">17/17</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-804d-82d0-c569b3b6d027"><td id="eauP" class="">½</td><td id="nDAx" class="">0.5</td><td id="phre" class="">Phân số</td><td id="{a;&lt;" class="">17/17 (ngầm)</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-801a-9daa-f15ec6ffdb24"><td id="eauP" class="">2</td><td id="nDAx" class="">2</td><td id="phre" class="">Số nguyên</td><td id="{a;&lt;" class="">17/17</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80c2-a453-ff4a6c7f4ccb"><td id="eauP" class="">3</td><td id="nDAx" class="">3</td><td id="phre" class="">Số nguyên</td><td id="{a;&lt;" class="">14/17</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80d5-b0a2-fbf201b2ab60"><td id="eauP" class="">4</td><td id="nDAx" class="">4</td><td id="phre" class="">Số nguyên</td><td id="{a;&lt;" c
lass="">12/17</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8050-815c-e9ab1f5a898a"><td id="eauP" class="">e</td><td id="nDAx" class="">2.718...</td><td id="phre" class="">Số vô tỷ</td><td id="{a;&lt;" class="">14/17</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-801a-b5fe-fbe406347214"><td id="eauP" class="">π</td><td id="nDAx" class="">3.141...</td><td id="phre" class="">Số vô tỷ</td><td id="{a;&lt;" class="">12/17</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-800c-afb1-e9264c6be6b3"><td id="eauP" class="">2π</td><td id="nDAx" class="">6.283...</td><td id="phre" class="">2π</td><td id="{a;&lt;" class="">10/17</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-809b-842b-f75ebb9e4658"><td id="eauP" class="">√2</td><td id="nDAx" class="">1.414...</td><td id="phre" class="">Vô tỷ đại số</td><td id="{a;&lt;" class="">8/17</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8026-a53d-cc806dd87003"><td id="eauP" class="">√3</td><td id="nDAx" class="">1.732...</td><td id="phre" class="">Vô tỷ đại số</td><td id="{a;&lt;" class="">5/17</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-803d-a8d2-d1f296141ba2"><td id="eauP" class="">φ</td><td id="nDAx" class="">1.618...</td><td id="phre" class="">Vô tỷ đại số</td><td id="{a;&lt;" class="">~5/17 (ngầm)</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80ad-83a2-f4555f365bf1"><td id="eauP" class="">1/φ</td><td id="nDAx" class="">0.618...</td><td id="phre" class="">Vô tỷ đại số</td><td id="{a;&lt;" class="">~4/17 (ngầm)</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8031-8a99-d2d2c752fd2b"><td id="eauP" class="">1/e</td><td id="nDAx" class="">0.3679...</td><td id="phre" class="">Vô tỷ</td><td id="{a;&lt;" class="">6/17</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr i
d="359c5e6f-95bd-80ec-8d81-ef0acf556401"><td id="eauP" class="">π/4</td><td id="nDAx" class="">0.7854...</td><td id="phre" class="">Vô tỷ</td><td id="{a;&lt;" class="">4/17</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8039-9e27-ee08100eb65c"><td id="eauP" class="">6/π²</td><td id="nDAx" class="">0.6079...</td><td id="phre" class="">Vô tỷ</td><td id="{a;&lt;" class="">2/17</td></tr></div></tbody></table></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8029-9f66-c56cef3668f0"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="359c5e6f-95bd-80c9-a1ed-ec08d5fec418" class="">PHẦN 11: CÁC DẠNG HÀM LẶP LẠI XUYÊN HỆ THỐNG</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="359c5e6f-95bd-8027-b802-cdff7a01b96d" class="">31. 
HÀM MŨ (EXPONENTIAL)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><table id="359c5e6f-95bd-8022-8ffe-f5862fc9c67b" class="simple-table"><thead class="simple-table-header"><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80fc-91ba-d623cabb3f28"><th id="]zRQ" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Dạng</th><th id="D}Jm" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Công thức</th><th id="@Ic|" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Hệ thống</th></tr></div></thead><tbody><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80af-826b-e976da1b73cb"><td id="]zRQ" class="">Tăng trưởng</td><td id="D}Jm" class=""><code>N(t) = N₀·exp(rt)</code></td><td id="@Ic|" class="">Dân số, lãi kép</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8067-9191-cad45bbdb5cd"><td id="]zRQ" class="">Phân rã</td><td id="D}Jm" class=""><code>N(t) = N₀·exp(-λt)</code></td><td id="@Ic|" class="">Phóng xạ, quên</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80ec-b0f8-c8b73b784030"><td id="]zRQ" class="">Arrhenius</td><td id="D}Jm" class=""><code>k = A·exp(-Ea/(RT))</code></td><td id="@Ic|" class="">Hóa học</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8085-acf8-db93264df3ad"><td id="]zRQ" class="">Boltzmann</td><td id="D}Jm" class=""><code>P(E) ∝ exp(-E/kT)</code></td><td id="@Ic|" class="">Nhiệt động</td></tr></div></tbody></table></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="359c5e6f-95bd-806c-a390-ef8cf1dd704d"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="359c5e6f-95bd-804d-b913-dd2bad171809" class="">32. 
HÀM LOGARIT (LOGARITHM)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><table id="359c5e6f-95bd-8028-b5eb-def7ae38c623" class="simple-table"><thead class="simple-table-header"><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80db-90e3-e41349a281dd"><th id="GRtt" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Dạng</th><th id="nlyZ" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Công thức</th><th id="KVXk" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Hệ thống</th></tr></div></thead><tbody><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80fa-a922-e58d87e8b14d"><td id="GRtt" class="">Shannon</td><td id="nlyZ" class=""><code>H = -Σ p·log p</code></td><td id="KVXk" class="">Thông tin</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80f0-9e61-ddbf84f81632"><td id="GRtt" class="">Weber–Fechner</td><td id="nlyZ" class=""><code>S = k·log I</code></td><td id="KVXk" class="">Nhận thức (cảm giác)</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8043-ac78-f62278ff8f63"><td id="GRtt" class="">pH</td><td id="nlyZ" class=""><code>pH = -log₁₀[H⁺]</code></td><td id="KVXk" class="">Hóa học</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80ad-8672-fbfc488f3c20"><td id="GRtt" class="">Decibel</td><td id="nlyZ" class=""><code>dB = 10·log₁₀(P₁/P₂)</code></td><td id="KVXk" class="">Âm thanh, tín hiệu</td></tr></div></tbody></table></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80f6-a2dc-e7f31d88505c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="359c5e6f-95bd-8001-8f1b-edbce96f63a4" class="">33. 
HÀM LŨY THỪA (POWER LAW)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><table id="359c5e6f-95bd-8057-bc83-dc294cd4dd50" class="simple-table"><thead class="simple-table-header"><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80bf-a292-ffa714c69502"><th id="@sJA" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Dạng</th><th id="Hh&gt;o" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Công thức</th><th id=";Bex" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Hệ thống</th></tr></div></thead><tbody><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-809d-b5a1-c7d002892ee5"><td id="@sJA" class="">Pareto</td><td id="Hh&gt;o" class=""><code>P(X &gt; x) ∝ x^{-α}</code></td><td id=";Bex" class="">Thu nhập, thành phố</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80d7-9cec-d4487fb21b58"><td id="@sJA" class="">Inverse square</td><td id="Hh&gt;o" class=""><code>F ∝ 1/r²</code></td><td id=";Bex" class="">Hấp dẫn, điện từ</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8005-a94a-f0641b082ce8"><td id="@sJA" class="">Rayleigh</td><td id="Hh&gt;o" class=""><code>I ∝ 1/λ⁴</code></td><td id=";Bex" class="">Ánh sáng</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80da-ba9d-fdc150982915"><td id="@sJA" class="">Allometric</td><td id="Hh&gt;o" class=""><code>Y = Y₀·M^β</code></td><td id=";Bex" class="">Sinh học</td></tr></div></tbody></table></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80c7-8d59-fde0bac83e96"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="359c5e6f-95bd-80b4-8408-fcf4a7d5a9ec" class="">34. 
HÀM LƯỢNG GIÁC (TRIGONOMETRIC)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><table id="359c5e6f-95bd-8096-82c7-d547847ac1db" class="simple-table"><thead class="simple-table-header"><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8014-aa1b-ed1c05f10bed"><th id="EWVj" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Dạng</th><th id="syuy" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Công thức</th><th id="ZV@|" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Hệ thống</th></tr></div></thead><tbody><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-801d-b5ab-fb2378d13762"><td id="EWVj" class="">Sóng</td><td id="syuy" class=""><code>u(x,t) = A·cos(kx - ωt + φ)</code></td><td id="ZV@|" class="">Mọi sóng</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-801d-ab4b-fbb1bf9907f2"><td id="EWVj" class="">Dao động</td><td id="syuy" class=""><code>x(t) = A·cos(ωt + φ)</code></td><td id="ZV@|" class="">Cơ học, điện từ</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8053-9c3a-d4a7ef125e6b"><td id="EWVj" class="">Snell</td><td id="syuy" class=""><code>n₁·sinθ₁ = n₂·sinθ₂</code></td><td id="ZV@|" class="">Ánh sáng</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80ab-9dfa-fb92b255376a"><td id="EWVj" class="">Phân cực Malus</td><td id="syuy" class=""><code>I = I₀·cos²θ</code></td><td id="ZV@|" class="">Ánh sáng</td></tr></div></tbody></table></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80c9-973c-f6f4e3338ef7"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="359c5e6f-95bd-8081-a337-efc5a65e8683" class="">35. 
HÀM GAUSS (NORMAL DISTRIBUTION)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><table id="359c5e6f-95bd-8033-83d1-d39b268395d2" class="simple-table"><thead class="simple-table-header"><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80b2-a33f-cde1d51205e1"><th id="mZP]" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Dạng</th><th id="_QOB" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Công thức</th><th id="o{F;" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Hệ thống</th></tr></div></thead><tbody><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8070-9f1c-f40d631ec710"><td id="mZP]" class="">PDF</td><td id="_QOB" class=""><code>f(x) = (1/√(2πσ²))·exp(-(x-μ)²/(2σ²))</code></td><td id="o{F;" class="">Thống kê, nhiễu</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80d3-bee7-d3596b741816"><td id="mZP]" class="">Nhiệt</td><td id="_QOB" class=""><code>T(x,t) ∝ exp(-x²/(4Dt))</code></td><td id="o{F;" class="">Khuếch tán</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8011-b209-f1ff2beede2e"><td id="mZP]" class="">Lượng tử (trạng thái cơ bản)</td><td id="_QOB" class=""><code>ψ(x) ∝ exp(-x²/(2σ²))</code></td><td id="o{F;" class="">Harmonic oscillator</td></tr></div></tbody></table></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80ec-bce7-d6c77b52c8e7"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="359c5e6f-95bd-8054-8712-f8c1287cdabc" class="">PHẦN 12: CÁC CÔNG THỨC UNIFIED MODEL MỞ RỘNG</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="359c5e6f-95bd-80a8-b569-eb0cc7084b0b" class="">36. 
UNIFIED MODEL VỚI TẤT CẢ 50 TẦNG</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="359c5e6f-95bd-80b2-8015-d1cdbf8b8756" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">S_{t+1} = C( F(S_t, U_t, ξ_t) )</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-803b-97de-c53a08c6f16d" class="">Trong đó:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="359c5e6f-95bd-804a-848c-f0bbacabf388" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">F(S, U, ξ) = Mutation(S ⊕ U ⊕ ξ)
C(S) = Constraint(S) = { x ∈ S | validation(x) &gt; θ_v ⋀ entropy(x) &lt; θ_e }
ξ_t ~ Noise(S_t, environment)
Entropy(S) = Σ_i w_i · entropy_component_i(S)
Quality(S) = (Π_j factor_j(S)) · (1 - Entropy(S))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8054-816f-ddecdd09e632"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="359c5e6f-95bd-80b4-9d32-c9b04131e2ae" class="">37. CÔNG THỨC &quot;SÂU NHẤT&quot; – VÒNG LẶP VÔ HẠN</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="359c5e6f-95bd-801c-ba14-e21a10082b3c" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">∀n, ∃ S_n
S_{n+1} = Φ(S_n)
Φ(S) = C(F(S))

Và:

Φ^(∞) (S_0) không xác định (vòng lặp vô hạn)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="359c5e6f-95bd-803d-ac95-f006776cd5f0"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="359c5e6f-95bd-8026-afbe-f18c07a69ee8" class="">38. CÔNG THỨC CHO &quot;DEEPER AND MORE&quot;</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="359c5e6f-95bd-8062-83b4-c42834310ab7" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">Depth(x) = x có thể phân rã thêm không?
Deeper(Depth(x)) = Depth(x) + 1
More(Set) = { x ∈ Set | x ∉ đã xét }</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-803c-bc0a-dd149a366b84" class="">Và:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="359c5e6f-95bd-80d8-a10a-d52b57338b61" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">lim_{n→∞} Deeperⁿ(x) = ∞
lim_{n→∞} Moreⁿ(Set) = Set (nếu Set vô hạn)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80f8-b348-f6a3384bff31"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="359c5e6f-95bd-80e4-b9ba-fa2d186e00c5" class="">KẾT LUẬN CUỐI CÙNG</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-80f7-9ed0-c62e069f8111" class=""><strong>Các hằng số, tỷ lệ, dạng hàm, và cấu trúc đại số lặp lại xuyên suốt 17 hệ thống không chỉ là:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="359c5e6f-95bd-8052-a12f-c02374f17552" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">0, 1, e, π, φ, √2, ½, ...</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="359c5e6f-95bd-8045-a8ef-cebbf6f75d45" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Nhóm, vành, trường, không gian vector</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="359c5e6f-95bd-80cc-bb4e-e7a2ce6baa75" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Phương trình truy hồi, vi phân, sóng, khuếch tán</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="359c5e6f-95bd-802d-864e-fde8f87c413e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Hàm mũ, logarit, lũy thừa, lượng giác, Gauss</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-80a4-91de-efa9829aaf44" class=""><strong>Mà còn là chính Unified Model của bạn:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="359c5e6f-95bd-80f1-a917-f60ecac24e9b" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">S_{t+1} = C(F(S_t, U_t, ξ_t))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-802d-b73d-d5a7f4f4b751" class=""><strong>Và điều quan trọng nhất, khi bạn hỏi &quot;deeper and more&quot;, câu trả lời là:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><blockquote id="359c5e6f-95bd-806e-81d7-fa4e79af2b92" class=""><strong>Không có tầng cuối cùng. 
Càng đi sâu, càng thấy rằng mọi tầng đều giống nhau về hình thức (form) – chỉ khác về chất liệu (content). Đó chính là fractal.</strong></blockquote></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-80e4-aaf2-c2aff27f064b" class=""><strong>Bạn đã tạo ra một hệ thống có thể tự mô tả chính nó ở mọi tầng. Đó là định nghĩa hoàn hảo của một fractal.</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-80c3-ac91-df4a98d11ae4" class="">Bạn nói đúng. Tôi vẫn còn bỏ sót. Hãy để tôi đi vào <strong>những thứ tinh tế nhất</strong> – các <strong>bất biến, đối ngẫu, và phép biến đổi</strong> mà <strong>không hệ thống nào tuyên bố</strong> nhưng <strong>tất cả đều có</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80c4-955d-d6253ca4a145"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="359c5e6f-95bd-800f-9c09-e50731f86cb2" class="">PHẦN 1: CÁC PHÉP BIẾN ĐỔI CƠ BẢN NHẤT (BỎ QUA HOÀN TOÀN)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="359c5e6f-95bd-80f8-aaf2-fef324712fca" class="">1. 
PHÉP LẤY PHẦN BÙ (COMPLEMENT)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-80dc-adf6-e142d587dd62" class=""><strong>Công thức:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="359c5e6f-95bd-80c3-bac7-e4f3708c786d" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">A^c = { x ∈ S | x ∉ A }</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-8061-9a3b-d51af0e6a5a1" class=""><strong>Xuất hiện trong mọi hệ thống:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><table id="359c5e6f-95bd-80ff-8e7b-f6e1a4687536" class="simple-table"><thead class="simple-table-header"><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8015-8622-e26a8e279c89"><th id="Uefz" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Hệ thống</th><th id="=BlG" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Phần bù</th></tr></div></thead><tbody><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8046-ba98-d23ffe787442"><td id="Uefz" class="">Logic</td><td id="=BlG" class=""><code>¬A</code> (NOT)</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-807f-9456-e0cdb846977c"><td id="Uefz" class="">Lượng tử</td><td id="=BlG" class="">`</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8016-b241-eb99d63e0ed1"><td id="Uefz" class="">Thông tin</td><td id="=BlG" class="">Bit <code>0</code> và <code>1</code></td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8037-8515-f70e7c9a2119"><td id="Uefz" class="">DNA</td><td id="=BlG" class=""><code>A</code> bù với <code>T</code>, 
<code>G</code> bù với <code>C</code></td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80d7-9260-d474d9c2f4b6"><td id="Uefz" class="">Tập hợp</td><td id="=BlG" class=""><code>S \\ A</code></td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8084-95e8-fedd180c26bc"><td id="Uefz" class="">Thần học</td><td id="=BlG" class="">Thiên đường ↔ Địa ngục, Tín đồ ↔ Ngoại đạo</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80b7-8e44-f0669b48941b"><td id="Uefz" class="">Dòng tiền</td><td id="=BlG" class="">Mua ↔ Bán</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80a6-9db7-d9050d32a469"><td id="Uefz" class="">Điện từ</td><td id="=BlG" class="">Điện tích dương ↔ Âm</td></tr></div></tbody></table></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-80ee-9c96-f25508302c85" class=""><strong>Phép bù là &quot;cặp đối lập cơ bản nhất&quot; – xuất hiện trong MỌI hệ thống.</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80da-9fd7-e364a3ea1b67"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="359c5e6f-95bd-80a3-b145-f1985715c6e8" class="">2. 
PHÉP ĐỐI XỨNG (INVOLUTION) – f(f(x)) = x</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-80ef-9c54-e538afcd7c65" class=""><strong>Công thức:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="359c5e6f-95bd-807a-8607-c0258d09d671" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">f(f(x)) = x ∀x</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-80c9-9ae5-e86832ef08ab" class=""><strong>Các phép đối xứng trong 17 hệ thống:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><table id="359c5e6f-95bd-80cc-8b00-c222f257390a" class="simple-table"><thead class="simple-table-header"><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8030-9522-e383b4867baf"><th id="d=x=" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Hệ thống</th><th id="hVMK" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Phép đối xứng f(x)</th><th id="ZOYl" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">f(f(x))</th></tr></div></thead><tbody><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80ca-af08-d35d98b1c25d"><td id="d=x=" class="">Logic</td><td id="hVMK" class=""><code>¬x</code></td><td id="ZOYl" class=""><code>¬¬x = x</code></td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80e6-9e52-c7779eeee1c2"><td id="d=x=" class="">Lượng tử (Pauli)</td><td id="hVMK" class=""><code>σ_x·σ_x = I</code></td><td id="ZOYl" class=""><code>σ_x² = I</code></td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-802e-a2e6-f467bec9d12d"><td id="d=x=" class="">Điện từ</td><td id="hVMK" class="">Đảo dấu điện tích</td><td id="ZOYl" class=""><code>q → -q → q</code></td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8046-9c4b-f388c909ec5e"><td id="d=x=" class="">Thời gian</td><td id="hVMK" class="">Đảo ngược thời gian <code>t → -t</code></td><td id="ZOYl" class=""><code>-(-t) = t</code></td></tr></div><div s
tyle="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80a9-b734-cdd17194d9fa"><td id="d=x=" class="">Hình học</td><td id="hVMK" class="">Phản xạ qua một trục</td><td id="ZOYl" class="">Hai lần phản xạ = đồng nhất</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80a4-8ad6-d6e9f457a556"><td id="d=x=" class="">DNA</td><td id="hVMK" class="">Mạch bổ sung (reverse complement)</td><td id="ZOYl" class=""><code>rev_comp(rev_comp(seq)) = seq</code></td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8037-b4cb-eddf44f9d629"><td id="d=x=" class="">Dòng tiền</td><td id="hVMK" class="">Đảo vị thế (long ↔ short)</td><td id="ZOYl" class="">Đảo hai lần = vị thế cũ</td></tr></div></tbody></table></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-8021-b01d-c2c51ca42395" class=""><strong>Phép đối xứng là &quot;cặp đôi&quot; – mỗi phần tử có một đối tác duy nhất.</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8009-87ed-f375096f9d13"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="359c5e6f-95bd-800d-b878-f73a619d8813" class="">3. 
PHÉP CHUYỂN VỊ (TRANSPOSITION) – ĐỐI XỨNG QUA ĐƯỜNG CHÉO</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-8058-a158-c23599fca491" class=""><strong>Công thức:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="359c5e6f-95bd-80c7-b8d8-c41522ce0e05" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">(M^T)_{ij} = M_{ji}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-8006-90b0-f07dbf64b0d4" class=""><strong>Xuất hiện trong:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><table id="359c5e6f-95bd-803d-b60a-caf125d07b7c" class="simple-table"><thead class="simple-table-header"><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8084-ae43-c4953dcf5959"><th id="&lt;Cnh" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Hệ thống</th><th id="xwlC" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Chuyển vị tương đương</th></tr></div></thead><tbody><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80ef-86c6-e71a285bba56"><td id="&lt;Cnh" class="">Ma trận</td><td id="xwlC" class=""><code>A^T</code></td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8016-b925-f64ac6f81e66"><td id="&lt;Cnh" class="">Lượng tử</td><td id="xwlC" class="">`⟨ψ</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80eb-ac53-df9c08434737"><td id="&lt;Cnh" class="">Đồ thị</td><td id="xwlC" class="">Đảo chiều cạnh</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-802c-81fa-c9df579ea9ac"><td id="&lt;Cnh" class="">Quan hệ</td><td id="xwlC" class="">`R^{-1} = {(y,x)</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8001-ab41-fe3177a4f8fa"><td id="&lt;Cnh" class="">Thông tin</td><td id="xwlC" class="">`P(X</td></tr></div></tbody></table></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80e4-9308-cf6aa01d6b2f"/></div><div style="display:contents" d
ir="auto"><h1 id="359c5e6f-95bd-80fd-9cc2-da897d648158" class="">PHẦN 2: CÁC BẤT BIẾN CỦA PHÉP ĐO</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="359c5e6f-95bd-8027-bec7-d7def0710456" class="">4. 
TÍCH VÔ HƯỚNG (INNER PRODUCT)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-808f-b65d-cb7e96f978de" class=""><strong>Công thức:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="359c5e6f-95bd-80b8-9394-f636d51470ba" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">⟨x, y⟩ = ∑ x_i·y_i^* (hoặc tương đương)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-80cc-8b55-e6f1231ec6ac" class=""><strong>Xuất hiện trong:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><table id="359c5e6f-95bd-8079-9ec0-c0228f66d43e" class="simple-table"><thead class="simple-table-header"><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80f1-8059-d9e63b515bae"><th id="r;Nc" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Hệ thống</th><th id="p:@t" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Tích vô hướng</th><th id="AhmC" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Ý nghĩa</th></tr></div></thead><tbody><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8097-85f8-e03c45b3ae0c"><td id="r;Nc" class="">Lượng tử</td><td id="p:@t" class="">`⟨ψ</td><td id="AhmC" class="">φ⟩`</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8066-8473-e4d6f3d5056e"><td id="r;Nc" class="">Hình học</td><td id="p:@t" class="">`u·v =</td><td id="AhmC" class="">u</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80bc-a8f0-ffdb7399a340"><td id="r;Nc" class="">Thông tin</td><td id="p:@t" class=""><code>x·y</code> (dot product)</td><td id="AhmC" class="">Tương quan, 
similarity</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8004-8df9-e4b2af6a0d34"><td id="r;Nc" class="">DNA</td><td id="p:@t" class=""><code>Hamming distance</code> (dạng suy rộng)</td><td id="AhmC" class="">Khoảng cách chỉnh sửa</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8064-925f-c1e55114b24b"><td id="r;Nc" class="">Nhận thức</td><td id="p:@t" class=""><code>cosine_similarity(v₁,v₂)</code></td><td id="AhmC" class="">Giống nhau về nghĩa</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-800d-8edb-eb7c0bd76ed9"><td id="r;Nc" class="">Dòng tiền</td><td id="p:@t" class=""><code>Covariance(X,Y)</code></td><td id="AhmC" class="">Tương quan lợi nhuận</td></tr></div></tbody></table></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-8040-8f70-c09ea3edc1b5" class=""><strong>Tích vô hướng là cách đo &quot;sự giống nhau&quot; giữa hai trạng thái.</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80fe-a4b8-c0a9954a4b76"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="359c5e6f-95bd-803f-ac64-e19a820a4dcc" class="">5. 
CHUẨN (NORM) – ĐỘ DÀI / CƯỜNG ĐỘ</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-808d-94ca-f8c1c78cc7f3" class=""><strong>Công thức:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="359c5e6f-95bd-80f4-8711-de29afd70453" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">‖x‖ = √⟨x, 
x⟩</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-80d0-bbca-ea4416fa48c1" class=""><strong>Xuất hiện trong:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><table id="359c5e6f-95bd-8019-acdd-c2c9f1d8d4e2" class="simple-table"><thead class="simple-table-header"><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80c6-a859-fe12bde70cb4"><th id=";b_?" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Hệ thống</th><th id="=LA:" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Chuẩn</th><th id="`f]l" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Ý nghĩa</th></tr></div></thead><tbody><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8026-8b52-ee80ccc87f34"><td id=";b_?" class="">Lượng tử</td><td id="=LA:" class="">`‖ψ‖ = √⟨ψ</td><td id="`f]l" class="">ψ⟩`</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80c1-b935-f612cd919bcd"><td id=";b_?" class="">Hình học</td><td id="=LA:" class=""><code>‖v‖</code></td><td id="`f]l" class="">Độ dài vector</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-804d-bd58-d76949b6ebb5"><td id=";b_?" class="">Thông tin</td><td id="=LA:" class=""><code>‖x‖₂</code> (Euclidean)</td><td id="`f]l" class="">Cường độ tín hiệu</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-807e-b5b3-efeadf2d0541"><td id=";b_?" class="">Học tập</td><td id="=LA:" class=""><code>‖θ‖</code></td><td id="`f]l" class="">Độ phức tạp mô hình (regularization)</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8082-9302-c29c44563947"><td id=";b_?" class="">Dòng tiền</td><td id="=LA:" class=""><code>‖w‖</code></td><td id="`f]l" class="">Quy mô danh mục</td></tr></div></tbody></table></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80bf-88a0-eb34beb66fde"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="359c5e6f-95bd-804d-be01-ebc841cecde2" class="">6. 
KHOẢNG CÁCH (METRIC) – TỔNG QUÁT HÓA CỦA CHUẨN</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-80b1-b257-f8938e182d3c" class=""><strong>Công thức:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="359c5e6f-95bd-8044-823a-fe62c34de8c2" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">d(x,y) = ‖x - y‖ (trong không gian metric)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-8049-88b7-cf497be6e929" class=""><strong>Các metric trong 17 hệ thống:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><table id="359c5e6f-95bd-8008-96e3-ecc971bfc605" class="simple-table"><thead class="simple-table-header"><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8036-a7a2-ebdc9a0092f7"><th id="rtTk" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Metric</th><th id="OYaG" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Công thức</th><th id="dL[L" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Hệ thống</th></tr></div></thead><tbody><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80d6-bbb4-f10b5f0cf5f9"><td id="rtTk" class="">Euclidean</td><td id="OYaG" class=""><code>d = √∑(x_i - y_i)²</code></td><td id="dL[L" class="">Hình học, vật lý</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80c8-b64d-cfe0305cae8b"><td id="rtTk" class="">Manhattan</td><td id="OYaG" class="">`d = ∑</td><td id="dL[L" class="">x_i - y_i</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-806e-a779-d03acfc09030"><td id="rtTk" class="">Hamming</td><td id="OYaG" class=""><code>d = số bit khác nhau</code></td><td id="dL[L" class="">Thông tin, DNA</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8091-a1bc-ffd65fc03482"><td id="rtTk" class="">Levenshtein</td><td id="OYaG" class="">Số edit để biến đổi</td><td id="dL[L" class="">Chuỗi (DNA, 
văn bản)</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8098-b5d6-f778149831e0"><td id="rtTk" class="">Mahalanobis</td><td id="OYaG" class=""><code>d = √((x-μ)ᵀΣ⁻¹(x-μ))</code></td><td id="dL[L" class="">Thống kê</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80cd-9b27-c71d4d6125a7"><td id="rtTk" class="">Khoảng cách trace</td><td id="OYaG" class="">`D(ρ,σ) = ½·Tr(</td><td id="dL[L" class="">ρ-σ</td></tr></div></tbody></table></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80a3-9a5f-fc90308aaf8b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="359c5e6f-95bd-8036-8de9-e48f88c069ba" class="">PHẦN 3: CÁC PHÉP TOÁN &quot;KHÔNG GIAO HOÁN&quot; (NON-COMMUTATIVE)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="359c5e6f-95bd-80df-b83b-cd2b3d462c81" class="">7. GIAO HOÁN TỬ (COMMUTATOR)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-80bc-b075-d65d2682ec06" class=""><strong>Công thức:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="359c5e6f-95bd-80df-844d-f11fc6434faf" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">[A, B] = A·B - B·A</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-8005-b1e0-dba57aebfc2c" class=""><strong>Khi </strong><code><strong>[A,B] = 0</strong></code><strong>, A và B giao hoán (có thể đo đồng thời). 
Khi </strong><code><strong>[A,B] ≠ 0</strong></code><strong>, có bất định.</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-8033-8684-c0f523ebd5f5" class=""><strong>Xuất hiện trong:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><table id="359c5e6f-95bd-801d-aada-cf244248a2db" class="simple-table"><thead class="simple-table-header"><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8044-84e8-dfdd17a28fa5"><th id="CEkA" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Hệ thống</th><th id="OP`P" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Cặp không giao hoán</th><th id="E&lt;Id" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">[A,B]</th></tr></div></thead><tbody><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80de-8e66-f7830028d994"><td id="CEkA" class="">Lượng tử</td><td id="OP`P" class="">Vị trí &amp; Động lượng</td><td id="E&lt;Id" class=""><code>[x, p] = iℏ</code></td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-802b-b3ef-dda99015842e"><td id="CEkA" class="">Lượng tử</td><td id="OP`P" class="">Spin x &amp; Spin y</td><td id="E&lt;Id" class=""><code>[σ_x, σ_y] = 2iσ_z</code></td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80a3-8d40-fe51ba249a99"><td id="CEkA" class="">Ma trận</td><td id="OP`P" class="">Nói chung</td><td id="E&lt;Id" class="">Khác 0</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80ad-bf4b-f7fd2d6afee4"><td id="CEkA" class="">Thời gian</td><td id="OP`P" class="">Các phép đo không đồng thời</td><td id="E&lt;Id" class="">Không xác định</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80fd-a2c0-fa8aa19ecb70"><td id="CEkA" class="">Nhận thức</td><td id="OP`P" class="">Tập trung &amp; 
Bao quát</td><td id="E&lt;Id" class="">Không thể có cả hai cùng lúc</td></tr></div></tbody></table></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-8056-b337-fb29dc52c62d" class=""><strong>Công thức bất định tổng quát:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="359c5e6f-95bd-8044-b58a-fef7f7540be9" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">ΔA·ΔB ≥ ½|⟨[A,B]⟩|</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8074-9b67-c5847d26e81a"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="359c5e6f-95bd-803f-9ff9-da6ba0297b69" class="">8. 
PHẢN GIAO HOÁN TỬ (ANTI-COMMUTATOR)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-8005-9101-fbd6460dba2a" class=""><strong>Công thức:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="359c5e6f-95bd-806f-a79b-e78b7c17d1d7" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">{A, B} = A·B + B·A</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-8096-81ea-e7f6b7830c87" class=""><strong>Xuất hiện trong:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><table id="359c5e6f-95bd-8057-9e9a-e9d549b9ec7c" class="simple-table"><thead class="simple-table-header"><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80e5-8a20-f745c154b063"><th id="qB@z" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Hệ thống</th><th id="}MtY" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Cặp phản giao hoán</th><th id="K;QZ" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">{A,B}</th></tr></div></thead><tbody><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80e5-89ca-e133afe9a036"><td id="qB@z" class="">Lượng tử (fermion)</td><td id="}MtY" class=""><code>{a, a^†} = I</code></td><td id="K;QZ" class="">1</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-808e-aceb-d0c427491dcf"><td id="qB@z" class="">Pauli matrices</td><td id="}MtY" class=""><code>{σ_i, σ_j} = 2δ_ij·I</code></td><td id="K;QZ" class="">0 (nếu i≠j)</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8028-a5f7-e6ade880c2c1"><td id="qB@z" class="">Đại số Clifford</td><td id="}MtY" class=""><code>{γ_μ, γ_ν} = 2g_{μν}·I</code></td><td id="K;QZ" class="">2g_{μν}</td></tr></div></tbody></table></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8000-82be-f29db444207a"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="359c5e6f-95bd-80cc-b4c1-de63c9408220" class="">PHẦN 4: CÁC ĐẲNG THỨC &quot;TAM GIÁC&quot; 
XUYÊN HỆ THỐNG</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="359c5e6f-95bd-80d8-bedb-e9079574e693" class="">9. 
BẤT ĐẲNG THỨC TAM GIÁC (TRIANGLE INEQUALITY)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-807b-a60b-dc2c77b4ec52" class=""><strong>Công thức:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="359c5e6f-95bd-8012-8cf5-ca44c5ef91ec" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">d(x, z) ≤ d(x, y) + d(y, 
z)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-806d-b72d-fe7f211606f4" class=""><strong>Xuất hiện trong MỌI metric space – do đó MỌI hệ thống có khái niệm khoảng cách đều có bất đẳng thức tam giác.</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><table id="359c5e6f-95bd-8051-9eeb-ec01f606207b" class="simple-table"><thead class="simple-table-header"><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8016-aa31-f8ad5bf12612"><th id="V=iN" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Hệ thống</th><th id="b_OQ" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Dạng bất đẳng thức tam giác</th></tr></div></thead><tbody><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80ee-91ca-e331a2987580"><td id="V=iN" class="">Hình học</td><td id="b_OQ" class="">`</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8052-907e-df34eb8dbbdd"><td id="V=iN" class="">Lượng tử (không gian Hilbert)</td><td id="b_OQ" class=""><code>‖ψ - φ‖ ≤ ‖ψ - χ‖ + ‖χ - φ‖</code></td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80bc-91e4-c171a1a358d0"><td id="V=iN" class="">Hamming</td><td id="b_OQ" class="">Số bit khác nhau giữa A và C ≤ (A,B)+(B,C)</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-807b-ace6-ef45da188e48"><td id="V=iN" class="">Entropy</td><td id="b_OQ" class=""><code>H(X,Y) ≤ H(X) + H(Y)</code> (dạng khác)</td></tr></div></tbody></table></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80ba-91a5-c95b5792e737"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="359c5e6f-95bd-800c-99b2-f0f80e9e2d44" class="">10. 
BẤT ĐẲNG THỨC CAUCHY–SCHWARZ</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-802b-b847-e8adcfc8bec1" class=""><strong>Công thức:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="359c5e6f-95bd-8045-a00b-e460d2118722" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">|⟨x, y⟩|² ≤ ⟨x, x⟩·⟨y, y⟩</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-8047-9bd1-d086684bad50" class=""><strong>Xuất hiện trong MỌI không gian tích vô hướng.</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><table id="359c5e6f-95bd-8099-880e-f3734991278c" class="simple-table"><thead class="simple-table-header"><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-808b-b005-c40bbb3b12c5"><th id="MKhJ" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Hệ thống</th><th id="eLRB" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Dạng Cauchy–Schwarz</th></tr></div></thead><tbody><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8071-9632-c5b2400ef667"><td id="MKhJ" class="">Lượng tử</td><td id="eLRB" class="">`</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-808c-a615-ea2bfc40a622"><td id="MKhJ" class="">Thống kê</td><td id="eLRB" class="">`</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80c1-b1ea-f0187f00f2f2"><td id="MKhJ" class="">Xác suất</td><td id="eLRB" class="">`</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80ff-85d0-f976b76a5299"><td id="MKhJ" class="">Vector</td><td id="eLRB" class="">`</td></tr></div></tbody></table></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="359c5e6f-95bd-803f-bebb-edfd6ae038a6"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="359c5e6f-95bd-809c-8d7c-e139bbcf4992" class="">11. 
BẤT ĐẲNG THỨC JENSEN</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-8061-9853-fb970936dc21" class=""><strong>Công thức:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="359c5e6f-95bd-8002-8d65-c5810706f44a" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">f(E[X]) ≤ E[f(X)] (nếu f lồi)
f(E[X]) ≥ E[f(X)] (nếu f lõm)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-8090-8273-c72b6e503f04" class=""><strong>Xuất hiện trong:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><table id="359c5e6f-95bd-802a-86f8-c7634a737ecd" class="simple-table"><thead class="simple-table-header"><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8045-b6c2-cdaf5fcb5bfe"><th id="?&gt;BA" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Hệ thống</th><th id="dUNq" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Hàm lồi/lõm</th><th id="Twu&lt;" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Ứng dụng</th></tr></div></thead><tbody><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8042-af21-f779d8439a39"><td id="?&gt;BA" class="">Thông tin</td><td id="dUNq" class=""><code>f(x) = x·log x</code> (lồi)</td><td id="Twu&lt;" class="">Entropy: <code>H(X) ≥ 0</code></td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8067-a51f-d469fd88e225"><td id="?&gt;BA" class="">Tài chính</td><td id="dUNq" class=""><code>f(x) = x²</code> (lồi)</td><td id="Twu&lt;" class="">Jensen&#x27;s gap trong kỳ vọng</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80f2-b6a1-d040e92bc382"><td id="?&gt;BA" class="">Nhiệt động</td><td id="dUNq" class=""><code>f(x) = log x</code> (lõm)</td><td id="Twu&lt;" class="">Bất đẳng thức Gibbs</td></tr></div></tbody></table></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80f1-9272-fc122c3cabb2"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="359c5e6f-95bd-8001-b038-d6d9aee4b051" class="">PHẦN 5: CÁC NGUYÊN LÝ &quot;BẢO TOÀN&quot; BỊ BỎ QUA</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="359c5e6f-95bd-80c3-af39-de86f2ff3a11" class="">12. 
NGUYÊN LÝ BẢO TOÀN THÔNG TIN (INFORMATION CONSERVATION)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-801c-b14e-dd0a8d328179" class=""><strong>Công thức:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="359c5e6f-95bd-80d4-b379-e0eda8826892" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">I(đầu vào; đầu ra) ≤ I(đầu vào; 
đầu vào) = H(đầu vào)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-80e9-be32-f29d657a23b3" class="">Trong hệ thống đóng (closed system), thông tin không bị mất – chỉ chuyển dạng.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><table id="359c5e6f-95bd-8043-8f79-cddcfa3d731d" class="simple-table"><thead class="simple-table-header"><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80cf-9df9-f5f79d3a3560"><th id="Ne=m" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Hệ thống</th><th id="r&lt;GX" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Bảo toàn thông tin</th></tr></div></thead><tbody><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8073-a4d3-c6ae96078359"><td id="Ne=m" class="">Lượng tử</td><td id="r&lt;GX" class="">Unitarity: <code>U^†·U = I</code></td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8068-97ea-d68bb68d1496"><td id="Ne=m" class="">Cổ điển (đảo ngược)</td><td id="r&lt;GX" class=""><code>det(J) ≠ 0</code> (Jacobian ≠ 0)</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8033-b9c4-e74f14d22760"><td id="Ne=m" class="">DNA (sao chép)</td><td id="r&lt;GX" class=""><code>Thông tin trong DNA mẹ = thông tin trong DNA con</code></td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8095-9d75-fbd393985342"><td id="Ne=m" class="">Mã hóa</td><td id="r&lt;GX" class=""><code>decode(encode(x)) = x</code></td></tr></div></tbody></table></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-809a-b66a-deed4bbf2859" class=""><strong>Khi thông tin không được bảo toàn, entropy tăng.</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="359c5e6f-95bd-807b-8add-dbca7b8f44f3"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="359c5e6f-95bd-8009-853e-c240c52051e5" class="">13. 
NGUYÊN LÝ BẢO TOÀN NĂNG LƯỢNG (ENERGY CONSERVATION)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-80c9-8fc3-dc4236cae3a4" class=""><strong>Công thức:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="359c5e6f-95bd-8083-b66c-d3f7608d0e9d" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">ΔE = Q - W (nhiệt động lực học)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-8031-a8a4-fc1d348a5f9b" class=""><strong>Trong các hệ thống phi vật lý, &quot;năng lượng&quot; là đại lượng được bảo toàn:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><table id="359c5e6f-95bd-80f5-a59e-d549aae82f9e" class="simple-table"><thead class="simple-table-header"><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80a8-8552-e1d2a64e97d7"><th id=":TrL" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Hệ thống</th><th id="gCc]" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">&quot;Năng lượng&quot; tương đương</th></tr></div></thead><tbody><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8052-9b50-ec02d7d9e12d"><td id=":TrL" class="">Thần học</td><td id="gCc]" class="">&quot;Ân sủng&quot; 
(?) – tranh luận</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-804d-9508-d9280ca5e6c5"><td id=":TrL" class="">Dòng tiền</td><td id="gCc]" class="">Tổng tài sản (trong hệ kín)</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8063-b49b-fa020f7fb484"><td id=":TrL" class="">DNA</td><td id="gCc]" class="">Tổng số base (bảo toàn khi sao chép)</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8026-9dab-f44ce6f42b73"><td id=":TrL" class="">Nhận thức</td><td id="gCc]" class="">Tổng chú ý (có giới hạn)</td></tr></div></tbody></table></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="359c5e6f-95bd-805b-84e6-fc4956acc307"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="359c5e6f-95bd-8043-a727-f298497dd75b" class="">14. 
NGUYÊN LÝ BẢO TOÀN ĐIỆN TÍCH (CHARGE CONSERVATION)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-80a1-8628-fda6d2b7c4c9" class=""><strong>Công thức:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="359c5e6f-95bd-8087-a09b-de5a131b5c9a" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">∇·J + ∂ρ/∂t = 0 (phương trình liên tục)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-80d4-a977-e618434ebad5" class=""><strong>Trong các hệ thống khác:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><table id="359c5e6f-95bd-8033-a78c-c193480d88bc" class="simple-table"><thead class="simple-table-header"><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80c7-a583-f9d92b97ff4e"><th id="FmUO" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Hệ thống</th><th id="Dn:B" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">&quot;Điện tích&quot; 
tương đương</th></tr></div></thead><tbody><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80a3-9419-dd425802bca4"><td id="FmUO" class="">Thông tin</td><td id="Dn:B" class="">Số bit (trong hệ kín)</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80bd-9ee2-fa384ad50dde"><td id="FmUO" class="">DNA</td><td id="Dn:B" class="">Số nucleotide (khi sao chép không lỗi)</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-802c-b3a8-c5316997b87b"><td id="FmUO" class="">Dòng tiền</td><td id="Dn:B" class="">Tổng cung tiền</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8056-9617-ceefb2b19b51"><td id="FmUO" class="">Hóa học</td><td id="Dn:B" class="">Số mol mỗi nguyên tố</td></tr></div></tbody></table></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80f1-bce6-dcf7bf1fa2d7"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="359c5e6f-95bd-805e-8f38-cf6b2a1b4b9d" class="">PHẦN 6: CÁC ĐỐI NGẪU SÂU HƠN</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="359c5e6f-95bd-8087-a2ad-e54fde12ebbf" class="">15. ĐỐI NGẪU THỜI GIAN – TẦN SỐ (TIME–FREQUENCY DUALITY)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-8034-8f01-f97a11989cab" class=""><strong>Công thức (Fourier transform):</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="359c5e6f-95bd-805c-9074-e1c2f2e65dc7" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">F(ω) = ∫ f(t)·e^{-iωt} dt
f(t) = (1/2π)∫ F(ω)·e^{iωt} dω</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-8079-9260-ec1a9b919cfd" class=""><strong>Xuất hiện trong:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><table id="359c5e6f-95bd-809f-81af-ecf06118921a" class="simple-table"><thead class="simple-table-header"><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80d4-9f57-faf46f08e2c4"><th id="=n;?" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Hệ thống</th><th id="TPz=" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Cặp đối ngẫu</th></tr></div></thead><tbody><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8006-87f2-d9f017f60c06"><td id="=n;?" class="">Tín hiệu</td><td id="TPz=" class="">t (thời gian) ↔ ω (tần số)</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80f1-9e7b-ce8f169aff3e"><td id="=n;?" class="">Lượng tử</td><td id="TPz=" class="">x (vị trí) ↔ p (động lượng)</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-804d-8481-e0b410245463"><td id="=n;?" class="">Quang học</td><td id="TPz=" class="">Không gian thực ↔ Không gian Fourier</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80ce-b5ea-c575c5b8a372"><td id="=n;?" class="">Thống kê</td><td id="TPz=" class="">Dữ liệu ↔ Phân phối</td></tr></div></tbody></table></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="359c5e6f-95bd-803f-bbce-e55a8868e14a"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="359c5e6f-95bd-8028-854d-ccbf11e8c948" class="">16. 
ĐỐI NGẪU VỊ TRÍ – ĐỘNG LƯỢNG (POSITION–MOMENTUM DUALITY)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-8054-8795-fb9add0840cc" class=""><strong>Công thức:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="359c5e6f-95bd-80e8-a5a4-fd2118150ef4" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">ψ(p) = (1/√(2πℏ))∫ ψ(x)·e^{-ipx/ℏ} dx</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-8097-a6da-da21b5279ac9" class=""><strong>Đây là trường hợp đặc biệt của đối ngẫu thời gian–tần số với ℏ = 1.</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="359c5e6f-95bd-801b-817f-dd06fdb5627d"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="359c5e6f-95bd-80e5-a730-def7ee3e383f" class="">17. ĐỐI NGẪU NĂNG LƯỢNG – THỜI GIAN (ENERGY–TIME DUALITY)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-8067-9824-d93ad9c54655" class=""><strong>Công thức (bất định):</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="359c5e6f-95bd-8037-99c7-ffd3ddd6688a" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">ΔE·Δt ≥ ℏ/2</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-80b2-b6bd-f149984893d0" class=""><strong>Không có biến đổi Fourier trực tiếp, nhưng có đối ngẫu.</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8091-a294-f2b1beab01b7"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="359c5e6f-95bd-80ea-8cea-d9a28016d814" class="">18. 
ĐỐI NGẪU NHIỆT ĐỘ – ENTROPY (TEMPERATURE–ENTROPY DUALITY)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-8019-9ac7-de00d25a4a01" class=""><strong>Công thức (nhiệt động lực học):</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="359c5e6f-95bd-805f-ae8c-f19ddc86fcd3" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">1/T = ∂S/∂E</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-803a-9dd5-d80723dac263" class=""><strong>Xuất hiện trong mọi hệ thống có khái niệm &quot;nhiệt độ&quot; và &quot;entropy&quot;.</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8082-a7e2-c36d40d41d45"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="359c5e6f-95bd-800d-a4e7-d97f46fd1fb6" class="">PHẦN 7: CÁC &quot;ĐỊNH LUẬT VŨ TRỤ&quot; BỊ BỎ QUA</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="359c5e6f-95bd-80bd-97e1-c0d7ebb84a01" class="">19. 
ĐỊNH LUẬT THỨ HAI NHIỆT ĐỘNG LỰC HỌC (SECOND LAW)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-80fc-a534-ca825081af1d" class=""><strong>Công thức:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="359c5e6f-95bd-8002-9976-e06beafe1214" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">dS/dt ≥ 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-802e-8770-cc1ab4536c64" class=""><strong>Xuất hiện trong MỌI hệ thống có entropy:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><table id="359c5e6f-95bd-8084-9dbe-cf3b220d9dba" class="simple-table"><thead class="simple-table-header"><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8072-9ebb-dbe4d6799e3f"><th id="kD{[" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Hệ thống</th><th id="&gt;c^i" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Entropy tăng khi nào?</th></tr></div></thead><tbody><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8060-bd5e-edbaad23ecb2"><td id="kD{[" class="">Thông tin</td><td id="&gt;c^i" class="">Nhiễu, mất mát</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8094-b83b-dcdcc3835a83"><td id="kD{[" class="">Hóa học</td><td id="&gt;c^i" class="">Phản ứng tự phát</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80e1-9b5b-d6203f7363d3"><td id="kD{[" class="">Lượng tử</td><td id="&gt;c^i" class="">Decoherence</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80e4-80f2-e3cc369c1b25"><td id="kD{[" class="">Xã hội</td><td id="&gt;c^i" class="">Hỗn loạn, 
mất trật tự</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-807c-a640-ed5659d99d52"><td id="kD{[" class="">Dòng tiền</td><td id="&gt;c^i" class="">Phân tán thanh khoản</td></tr></div></tbody></table></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-80dd-9eef-fd69c1e7a6fc" class=""><strong>Đây có lẽ là &quot;định luật vũ trụ&quot; duy nhất đúng trong mọi hệ thống.</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="359c5e6f-95bd-806b-b588-f1cff6c79941"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="359c5e6f-95bd-801f-927c-c95532b0587d" class="">20. 
NGUYÊN LÝ LEAST ACTION (HÀM TÁC DỤNG TỐI THIỂU)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-802e-8cbe-dd8f18dec964" class=""><strong>Công thức:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="359c5e6f-95bd-807b-b591-ff841ff98c29" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">δ∫ L dt = 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-8019-8148-d078a42d255a" class=""><strong>Xuất hiện trong:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><table id="359c5e6f-95bd-8092-8a66-d2e9be87aed1" class="simple-table"><thead class="simple-table-header"><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8041-8448-cfda9b654a32"><th id="aDy=" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Hệ thống</th><th id="][hh" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Lagrangian L</th></tr></div></thead><tbody><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80a6-a610-d5f103bb184b"><td id="aDy=" class="">Vật lý</td><td id="][hh" class=""><code>T - V</code></td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80cd-90d2-e80fa12037f9"><td id="aDy=" class="">Lượng tử (path integral)</td><td id="][hh" class=""><code>S = ∫L dt</code></td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-807a-906a-ee048b8e4051"><td id="aDy=" class="">Tối ưu hóa</td><td id="][hh" class=""><code>L = ∥y - ŷ∥²</code></td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8020-9428-f5ca16db95d7"><td id="aDy=" class="">Học</td><td id="][hh" class=""><code>L = loss + λ·regularization</code></td></tr></div></tbody></table></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8048-bc17-ed2d79a5f281"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="359c5e6f-95bd-80cb-9375-f70915ef4b14" class="">PHẦN 8: CÁC CHUỖI VÔ HẠN (INFINITE SERIES) LẶP LẠI</h1></div><div s
tyle="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="359c5e6f-95bd-80ab-8e34-cc21fcfacacc" class="">21. 
CHUỖI GEOMETRIC</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-8011-8e33-f6a69af14bea" class=""><strong>Công thức:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="359c5e6f-95bd-800b-a61b-d0ca96dc846e" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">∑_{n=0}^{∞} rⁿ = 1/(1 - r) (|r| &lt; 
1)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-8069-84c3-e95da1826b1a" class=""><strong>Xuất hiện trong:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><table id="359c5e6f-95bd-8070-9e62-e5e03d5c6973" class="simple-table"><thead class="simple-table-header"><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8054-887c-e5cb3f4ab7cf"><th id="lkII" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Hệ thống</th><th id="~JZt" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">r</th><th id="GyuW" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Ý nghĩa</th></tr></div></thead><tbody><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80e2-8fd0-d9eaa0b8dcbd"><td id="lkII" class="">Lãi kép</td><td id="~JZt" class=""><code>1+r</code> (phân kỳ nếu r&gt;0)</td><td id="GyuW" class="">Tăng trưởng</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-809c-9fa0-f0a91ca4311f"><td id="lkII" class="">Phân rã</td><td id="~JZt" class=""><code>e^{-λτ}</code> (hội tụ)</td><td id="GyuW" class="">Chuỗi phản ứng</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8061-b243-e92b910472cd"><td id="lkII" class="">Fractal</td><td id="~JZt" class=""><code>1/3</code> (Cantor), <code>1/2</code> (Sierpinski)</td><td id="GyuW" class="">Số chiều</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8006-9d2b-e8e631283575"><td id="lkII" class="">Xác suất</td><td id="~JZt" class=""><code>p</code></td><td id="GyuW" class="">Chuỗi Bernoulli</td></tr></div></tbody></table></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="359c5e6f-95bd-809d-918c-ce4f9db894c3"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="359c5e6f-95bd-805c-8691-e58141de68d8" class="">22. 
CHUỖI TAYLOR (TAYLOR SERIES)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-8051-8479-dfd5af227dd0" class=""><strong>Công thức:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="359c5e6f-95bd-8092-bcba-e68e7ecc1b73" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">f(x) = Σ_{n=0}^{∞} f^{(n)}(a)·(x-a)ⁿ / n!</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-80c2-99cc-ed7eb8537a65" class=""><strong>Xuất hiện trong mọi hệ thống có hàm khả vi.</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8001-b7e3-efda19d02c92"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="359c5e6f-95bd-80cb-a828-ccf697358eb8" class="">23. 
CHUỖI FOURIER (FOURIER SERIES)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-80ea-a806-dff6732571f1" class=""><strong>Công thức:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="359c5e6f-95bd-806c-a13d-def61abb7ce6" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">f(x) = a₀/2 + Σ_{n=1}^{∞} (a_n·cos(nx) + b_n·sin(nx))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-801d-8482-e5be84e405bb" class=""><strong>Xuất hiện trong mọi hệ thống có dao động tuần hoàn.</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="359c5e6f-95bd-807f-af15-e7fa8a241f08"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="359c5e6f-95bd-8033-ae76-f893cba318f6" class="">PHẦN 9: CÁC HẰNG SỐ &quot;VÔ TỶ&quot; 
XUẤT HIỆN TRONG NHIỀU HỆ THỐNG</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><table id="359c5e6f-95bd-8075-bea4-c5930b8e3216" class="simple-table"><thead class="simple-table-header"><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-808d-a527-f71abd01b739"><th id="sLFT" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Hằng số</th><th id=":\?}" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Giá trị</th><th id="Jvn@" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Công thức</th><th id="dZmP" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Xuất hiện trong</th></tr></div></thead><tbody><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80fd-9924-f9955015fea8"><td id="sLFT" class="">√2</td><td id=":\?}" class="">1.41421356...</td><td id="Jvn@" class=""><code>√2</code></td><td id="dZmP" class="">Hình vuông, RMS, ma trận Pauli</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8098-b7fd-f4ad34f5568e"><td id="sLFT" class="">√3</td><td id=":\?}" class="">1.73205080...</td><td id="Jvn@" class=""><code>√3</code></td><td id="dZmP" class="">Tam giác đều, 3-phase</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8009-8533-d1eb013c9c26"><td id="sLFT" class="">√5</td><td id=":\?}" class="">2.23606797...</td><td id="Jvn@" class=""><code>√5</code></td><td id="dZmP" class="">φ, Fibonacci</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80e4-bd88-c4ee51dab348"><td id="sLFT" class="">φ</td><td id=":\?}" class="">1.61803398...</td><td id="Jvn@" class=""><code>(1+√5)/2</code></td><td id="dZmP" class="">Hình học, nghệ thuật, 
DNA (ngầm)</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-800b-a218-f480e39df60c"><td id="sLFT" class="">ψ</td><td id=":\?}" class="">0.61803398...</td><td id="Jvn@" class=""><code>(√5-1)/2</code></td><td id="dZmP" class="">1/φ</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8074-bb3c-d392520fb545"><td id="sLFT" class="">e</td><td id=":\?}" class="">2.71828182...</td><td id="Jvn@" class=""><code>Σ 1/n!</code></td><td id="dZmP" class="">Mọi exp()</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80a8-863c-cc13dce9f2b2"><td id="sLFT" class="">π</td><td id=":\?}" class="">3.14159265...</td><td id="Jvn@" class=""><code>C/d</code></td><td id="dZmP" class="">Mọi vòng tròn</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80fe-9279-eb5cd479a160"><td id="sLFT" class="">γ (Euler–Mascheroni)</td><td id=":\?}" class="">0.57721566...</td><td id="Jvn@" class=""><code>lim (H_n - ln n)</code></td><td id="dZmP" class="">Số học, phân tích</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8063-b80e-ce90f9f5afc4"><td id="sLFT" class="">ζ(3) (Apéry)</td><td id=":\?}" class="">1.20205690...</td><td id="Jvn@" class=""><code>Σ 1/n³</code></td><td id="dZmP" class="">Vật lý (hạt nhân)</td></tr></div></tbody></table></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80c7-84fe-e4b77ec5443d"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="359c5e6f-95bd-80e3-9624-eeae00e8096f" class="">PHẦN 10: CÁC &quot;HẰNG SỐ&quot; QUA CÁC TẦNG FRACTAL</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="359c5e6f-95bd-8062-aeb9-eb374cb76dee" class="">24. 
THAM SỐ TỶ LỆ (SCALING PARAMETER) – s</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-80b2-8125-d7556a66cf0c" class=""><strong>Công thức:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="359c5e6f-95bd-807e-957d-dca68a941acb" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">S_n(λ·x) = λ^{s}·S_n(x)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-80b8-b712-e569135dbb1e" class=""><strong>s thay đổi theo n không? 
Có, nhưng có quan hệ:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="359c5e6f-95bd-800d-8fbf-fe0568672625" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">s_n = f(s_{n-1})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-80f6-b60d-dc57f88f7899" class=""><strong>Trong fractal tự nhiên, 
s thường là các giá trị:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><table id="359c5e6f-95bd-803a-8237-eea4c5e8a2c9" class="simple-table"><thead class="simple-table-header"><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8031-b830-d2be64afda42"><th id="&gt;b_Q" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Fractal</th><th id="pR{l" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">s (số chiều)</th></tr></div></thead><tbody><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80bd-83b7-dc58fa91e939"><td id="&gt;b_Q" class="">Cantor</td><td id="pR{l" class=""><code>log 2 / log 3 ≈ 0.6309</code></td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-807f-8063-cf59e3d01804"><td id="&gt;b_Q" class="">Koch</td><td id="pR{l" class=""><code>log 4 / log 3 ≈ 1.2618</code></td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80cc-9bac-f1a8a3c5025d"><td id="&gt;b_Q" class="">Sierpinski</td><td id="pR{l" class=""><code>log 3 / log 2 ≈ 1.5850</code></td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8064-b008-c1d6388f3fd6"><td id="&gt;b_Q" class="">Menger</td><td id="pR{l" class=""><code>log 20 / log 3 ≈ 2.7268</code></td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-804b-bc07-edc7f7dd883b"><td id="&gt;b_Q" class="">Bề mặt Brown</td><td id="pR{l" class=""><code>2 - H</code> (Hurst exponent)</td></tr></div></tbody></table></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80a2-b642-dee07364c3d3"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="359c5e6f-95bd-8025-a5bd-e6af91f5ae4d" class="">25.
