BIẾN ĐỔI LAPLACE (Laplace Transform)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-8006-abc0-d8d896c26952" class=""><strong>Công thức:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="359c5e6f-95bd-80a0-9db0-ff0e1cfea581" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">F(s) = ∫_0^{∞} f(t)·e^{-st} dt</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-80c5-af17-e8f5cf196946" class=""><strong>Xuất hiện trong:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><table id="359c5e6f-95bd-804c-9395-f5850e621dd2" class="simple-table"><thead class="simple-table-header"><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80bc-b94d-f5fd0226a8fd"><th id="kbTp" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Hệ thống</th><th id="D{EI" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">f(t)</th><th id="cQ|u" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">F(s)</th></tr></div></thead><tbody><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80cb-872c-ce26935f1fb6"><td id="kbTp" class="">Điều khiển</td><td id="D{EI" class="">Tín hiệu</td><td id="cQ|u" class="">Hàm truyền</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8074-aac7-f245bdb94812"><td id="kbTp" class="">Mạch điện</td><td id="D{EI" class="">Dòng/áp</td><td id="cQ|u" class="">Trở kháng</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8066-9a98-f4993490e1cb"><td id="kbTp" class="">Sinh học</td><td id="D{EI" class="">Dược động học</td><td id="cQ|u" class="">Nồng độ theo thời gian</td></tr></div></tbody></table></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80ea-8c36-e3997dc7b311"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="359c5e6f-95bd-807d-9e21-c828716d00d1" class="">19. 
BIẾN ĐỔI WAVELET</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-80eb-bc58-c1e6807feb55" class=""><strong>Công thức:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="359c5e6f-95bd-80fc-810a-d42b0525ccab" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">W(a,b) = (1/√a)∫ f(t)·ψ((t-b)/a) dt</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-80ec-81a9-ccde3cc60532" class=""><strong>Xuất hiện trong:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><table id="359c5e6f-95bd-802f-8b8b-d1aab5e9f8ca" class="simple-table"><thead class="simple-table-header"><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8057-8a90-db3b182c5427"><th id="X;K~" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Hệ thống</th><th id="j~G^" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Ứng dụng</th></tr></div></thead><tbody><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8055-9891-c02dce6c872b"><td id="X;K~" class="">Xử lý ảnh</td><td id="j~G^" class="">Nén JPEG2000</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80f4-b25e-f950f2634e75"><td id="X;K~" class="">Địa chấn</td><td id="j~G^" class="">Phân tích tín hiệu</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80b2-92d9-d737d6cdc18e"><td id="X;K~" class="">DNA</td><td id="j~G^" class="">Phân tích trình tự</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80a4-b0d9-da41db000a2d"><td id="X;K~" class="">Thị trường</td><td id="j~G^" class="">Phân tích đa thang đo</td></tr></div></tbody></table></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="359c5e6f-95bd-807a-b313-c1727acb6b2c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="359c5e6f-95bd-8044-b7e2-ed00a90c0b7d" class="">PHẦN 8: CÁC ĐẲNG THỨC TỔ HỢP (Combinatorial Identities)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 i
d="359c5e6f-95bd-8076-bfd4-f7bd0c35f95a" class="">20. NHỊ THỨC NEWTON (Binomial Theorem)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-8033-849b-eae9352cfe41" class=""><strong>Công thức:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="359c5e6f-95bd-8099-8765-e173ab32e575" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">(x + y)^n = ∑_{k=0}^{n} C(n,k)·x^k·y^{n-k}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-804b-bcd7-c221ac1387c5" class=""><strong>Xuất hiện trong:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><table id="359c5e6f-95bd-80c4-b2f7-edbed69e5a6b" class="simple-table"><thead class="simple-table-header"><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80a3-a36f-d69c6817b886"><th id="^S};" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Hệ thống</th><th id="IG]V" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">x, y</th><th id="yyHv" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">n</th></tr></div></thead><tbody><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8003-83b7-d956f5a9b97d"><td id="^S};" class="">Xác suất</td><td id="IG]V" class="">p, 1-p</td><td id="yyHv" class="">Số lần thử</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8018-9789-db1d510bce9e"><td id="^S};" class="">DNA</td><td id="IG]V" class="">A+T, G+C</td><td id="yyHv" class="">Độ dài</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80c3-8561-e711b4610cea"><td id="^S};" class="">Lượng tử</td><td id="IG]V" class=""></td><td id="yyHv" class="">0⟩,</td></tr></div></tbody></table></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80fb-89f3-cb40ec4b4493"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="359c5e6f-95bd-80f4-96d6-da37b08d4f55" class="">21. 
CÔNG THỨC STIRLING</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-8010-ba4f-df5901681f95" class=""><strong>Công thức:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="359c5e6f-95bd-8012-bb4a-f298c804b984" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">n! ∼ √(2πn)·(n/e)^n</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-8058-9bb5-d0664ce02a54" class=""><strong>Xuất hiện trong:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><table id="359c5e6f-95bd-8081-8532-d5af06119e7a" class="simple-table"><thead class="simple-table-header"><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80e5-aa9b-e37264d748e4"><th id="QoWf" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Hệ thống</th><th id="kHxK" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Ứng dụng</th></tr></div></thead><tbody><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-807c-8dcb-c44c948b4f23"><td id="QoWf" class="">Entropy</td><td id="kHxK" class=""><code>log n! ≈ n·log n - n</code></td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-806d-98da-d8ccb908cc8f"><td id="QoWf" class="">Thống kê</td><td id="kHxK" class="">Số cách sắp xếp</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8025-9e4e-f1dcf16189d5"><td id="QoWf" class="">Nhiệt động</td><td id="kHxK" class="">Số trạng thái vi mô</td></tr></div></tbody></table></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8097-bd8e-d843a99a96d5"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="359c5e6f-95bd-8047-9528-c6a07aba554d" class="">22. 
SỐ STIRLING LOẠI 2</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-8044-bc4b-f88f2894db0e" class=""><strong>Công thức:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="359c5e6f-95bd-805b-bb46-c0c9c99e194a" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">S(n,k) = số cách chia n phần tử vào k tập con không rỗng</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-8014-9b88-d246f24c2891" class=""><strong>Xuất hiện trong:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><table id="359c5e6f-95bd-8070-9098-c38e1ac1280c" class="simple-table"><thead class="simple-table-header"><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80ed-a69d-c0de5746d1fa"><th id="u&lt;Hy" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Hệ thống</th><th id="fgDq" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">n</th><th id="^I_}" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">k</th></tr></div></thead><tbody><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-805d-882c-f83d39ee6975"><td id="u&lt;Hy" class="">Phân cụm</td><td id="fgDq" class="">Số điểm</td><td id="^I_}" class="">Số cụm</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80c6-937b-f316653e91cd"><td id="u&lt;Hy" class="">Học máy</td><td id="fgDq" class="">Số mẫu</td><td id="^I_}" class="">Số lớp</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-805c-955c-f0f105745907"><td id="u&lt;Hy" class="">Hóa học</td><td id="fgDq" class="">Số phân tử</td><td id="^I_}" class="">Số pha</td></tr></div></tbody></table></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="359c5e6f-95bd-809e-b20f-cba8b2973d7f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="359c5e6f-95bd-809a-8efa-df9ea755eac2" class="">PHẦN 9: CÁC BẤT ĐẲNG THỨC TỔNG QUÁT</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="359c5e6f-95bd-8087-a979-d028812e61a5" c
lass="">23. BẤT ĐẲNG THỨC MINKOWSKI</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-80eb-8ad8-c04197115c4b" class=""><strong>Công thức:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="359c5e6f-95bd-8023-b6e8-e5e34b55178b" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">(∑|x_i + y_i|^p)^{1/p} ≤ (∑|x_i|^p)^{1/p} + (∑|y_i|^p)^{1/p}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-80e0-9287-eb4e7a526de2" class=""><strong>p=2: bất đẳng thức tam giác trong không gian Euclid.</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80a1-a9a2-fedd4d9a2922"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="359c5e6f-95bd-805c-87e9-e6f6a02a14fb" class="">24. BẤT ĐẲNG THỨC HÖLDER</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-8034-8d6a-c5465cf73c42" class=""><strong>Công thức:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="359c5e6f-95bd-8046-9cdf-c4d974572f48" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">∑|x_i·y_i| ≤ (∑|x_i|^p)^{1/p}·(∑|y_i|^q)^{1/q} (với 1/p + 1/q = 1)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-8077-a71b-d5cbbd1a6140" class=""><strong>Trường hợp đặc biệt p=2, q=2: Cauchy–Schwarz.</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="359c5e6f-95bd-800f-bfa7-fbc5f541f931"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="359c5e6f-95bd-8021-a2c8-dfc538140494" class="">25. 
BẤT ĐẲNG THỨC MARKOV</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-8093-af3c-cd44f8e9048d" class=""><strong>Công thức:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="359c5e6f-95bd-8089-8a2b-debe96331513" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">P(X ≥ a) ≤ E[X]/a</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-8079-ab1f-ed45b1f55021" class=""><strong>Xuất hiện trong mọi hệ thống có xác suất:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><table id="359c5e6f-95bd-801a-ac04-e04f2b5db4cb" class="simple-table"><thead class="simple-table-header"><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80bc-85c7-ee67b3243abb"><th id="pV}l" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Hệ thống</th><th id="HD[=" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">X</th><th id="z|CL" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">a</th></tr></div></thead><tbody><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80eb-89e2-d085deb648f3"><td id="pV}l" class="">Chất lượng</td><td id="HD[=" class="">lỗi</td><td id="z|CL" class="">ngưỡng</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8047-a96d-f5f3d1a542b0"><td id="pV}l" class="">Dòng tiền</td><td id="HD[=" class="">lỗ</td><td id="z|CL" class="">vốn</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80b4-bae6-dde8238f89f9"><td id="pV}l" class="">Lượng tử</td><td id="HD[=" class="">năng lượng</td><td id="z|CL" class="">kT</td></tr></div></tbody></table></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="359c5e6f-95bd-809a-a94a-d914288e9732"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="359c5e6f-95bd-809b-9b36-f79813ffdeaf" class="">26. 
BẤT ĐẲNG THỨC CHEBYSHEV</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-80ba-b8cc-c3fa70a5a993" class=""><strong>Công thức:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="359c5e6f-95bd-8091-8f48-e09d36872cf4" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">P(|X - μ| ≥ kσ) ≤ 1/k²</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-80c6-98da-e6dad3660671" class=""><strong>Không cần biết phân bố!</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="359c5e6f-95bd-802d-a103-c85de163d5b8"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="359c5e6f-95bd-8089-9402-fa7ffd841c06" class="">PHẦN 10: CÁC CHUỖI HÀM (Function Series) XUẤT HIỆN TRONG NHIỀU HỆ THỐNG</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="359c5e6f-95bd-8083-bd33-c2092ea5be09" class="">27. 
CHUỖI HÀM ĐIỀU HÒA (Harmonic Series)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-80a0-a483-ebaac4fdfc59" class=""><strong>Công thức:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="359c5e6f-95bd-8056-8638-ef870bc4f4ca" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">H_n = ∑_{k=1}^{n} 1/k</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-80d3-8add-fd00aab2a4d7" class=""><strong>Xuất hiện trong:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><table id="359c5e6f-95bd-80fe-ad37-c01bb6582957" class="simple-table"><thead class="simple-table-header"><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-802a-b7b4-f1dc6387894b"><th id="~]~&gt;" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Hệ thống</th><th id="VNby" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Ứng dụng</th></tr></div></thead><tbody><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80f2-bc2d-e088f0067318"><td id="~]~&gt;" class="">Phân tích</td><td id="VNby" class="">Số điều hòa</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80a7-b049-d2ede4356006"><td id="~]~&gt;" class="">Số học</td><td id="VNby" class="">≈ ln n + γ</td></tr></div></tbody></table></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80e0-a2f4-febd255004f8"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="359c5e6f-95bd-8034-b381-f01ed50ebc79" class="">28. 
CHUỖI HÀM MŨ (Exponential Series)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-8098-b8de-da66d5f0acfa" class=""><strong>Công thức:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="359c5e6f-95bd-8020-8be5-fa9749c8760b" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">e^x = ∑_{n=0}^{∞} x^n/n!</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-80c2-a383-fce68867e7d2" class=""><strong>Xuất hiện trong MỌI hệ thống có exp().</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8035-880f-c76bef699d69"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="359c5e6f-95bd-8037-9ca8-cb284aa771b6" class="">29. CHUỖI HÀM LOG (Logarithmic Series)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-8052-9b76-ff2be1422ac5" class=""><strong>Công thức:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="359c5e6f-95bd-809b-8dd1-d9e10575f2e9" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">ln(1+x) = ∑_{n=1}^{∞} (-1)^{n-1}·x^n/n</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8082-91aa-dd069ec3e61a"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="359c5e6f-95bd-80b9-a040-f49ff90c6e8e" class="">30. CHUỖI HÀM LƯỢNG GIÁC (Trigonometric Series)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-8038-bb57-cdf2c7c6c903" class=""><strong>Công thức:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="359c5e6f-95bd-8025-9fc7-e2148edfbe37" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">sin x = ∑_{n=0}^{∞} (-1)^n·x^{2n+1}/(2n+1)!
cos x = ∑_{n=0}^{∞} (-1)^n·x^{2n}/(2n)!</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8008-8107-e314710b72c8"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="359c5e6f-95bd-80cb-8654-daa8bd589f01" class="">PHẦN 11: CÁC &quot;HẰNG SỐ&quot; CỦA LÝ THUYẾT TRÒ CHƠI (Game Theory)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="359c5e6f-95bd-806f-8339-f4836cd928be" class="">31. 
GIÁ TRI TRÒ CHƠI (Game Value)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-807b-a5e6-f51ef073ad7c" class=""><strong>Công thức (von Neumann):</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="359c5e6f-95bd-800e-8607-c56af8c53c70" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">V = max_{x} min_{y} x^T·A·y = min_{y} max_{x} x^T·A·y</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-807a-829a-d5792d923dd3" class=""><strong>Xuất hiện trong:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><table id="359c5e6f-95bd-8056-aa04-e86b16fd629f" class="simple-table"><thead class="simple-table-header"><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8095-80de-e70c58323952"><th id="exi;" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Hệ thống</th><th id="&gt;pqC" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Ma trận A</th><th id="?s}D" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">V</th></tr></div></thead><tbody><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8069-8718-f4ed0753d67c"><td id="exi;" class="">Thị trường</td><td id="&gt;pqC" class="">Lợi nhuận</td><td id="?s}D" class="">Giá cân bằng</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-804d-858e-e652378efba0"><td id="exi;" class="">Đàm phán</td><td id="&gt;pqC" class="">Lợi ích</td><td id="?s}D" class="">Thỏa thuận</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8025-8925-f7d37ea1f2b4"><td id="exi;" class="">Tiến hóa</td><td id="&gt;pqC" class="">Độ thích nghi</td><td id="?s}D" class="">Chiến lược ổn định (ESS)</td></tr></div></tbody></table></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80e1-bf78-cfa6057d5b43"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="359c5e6f-95bd-8012-a8fc-ee5dcae703e0" class="">32. 
SHAPLEY VALUE (Đóng góp biên)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-808d-957c-dbd76c625ef5" class=""><strong>Công thức:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="359c5e6f-95bd-808b-ada2-d2d94cdbc310" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">φ_i(v) = (1/n!) ∑_{S⊆N\\{i}} (|S|!·(n-|S|-1)!·(v(S∪{i}) - v(S))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-8008-a29d-d8f8e310c644" class=""><strong>Xuất hiện trong:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><table id="359c5e6f-95bd-8091-a0ec-c953cea6bfa6" class="simple-table"><thead class="simple-table-header"><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-801c-a7e2-c352e862e43a"><th id="\Y^o" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Hệ thống</th><th id="Hng\" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">v(S)</th><th id="Bk?c" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">φ_i</th></tr></div></thead><tbody><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80f0-93de-cfbca68136bd"><td id="\Y^o" class="">Liên minh</td><td id="Hng\" class="">Giá trị liên minh</td><td id="Bk?c" class="">Đóng góp của i</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-806f-8a0b-c3148015665e"><td id="\Y^o" class="">Học máy</td><td id="Hng\" class="">Lợi ích mô hình</td><td id="Bk?c" class="">Tầm quan trọng feature</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80c9-9ae4-cad06880f85c"><td id="\Y^o" class="">DNA</td><td id="Hng\" class="">Hàm fitness</td><td id="Bk?c" class="">Đóng góp của gene</td></tr></div></tbody></table></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8043-9428-c22dcd64107d"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="359c5e6f-95bd-809f-86c4-c3f8b872ebc6" class="">PHẦN 12: BẢNG TỔNG HỢP &quot;MỌI THỨ&quot; 
(THE ULTIMATE TABLE)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><table id="359c5e6f-95bd-80de-b434-f7792648caf5" class="simple-table"><thead class="simple-table-header"><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8064-80ec-d243d5bdfd96"><th id="^CcP" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Danh mục</th><th id="^Osk" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Ví dụ</th><th id="O\m=" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Số hệ thống</th></tr></div></thead><tbody><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80ac-8cc3-dfec78665f36"><td id="^CcP" class="">Phép bù</td><td id="^Osk" class=""><code>A^c</code>, 
<code>¬A</code></td><td id="O\m=" class="">17/17</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8010-9003-c427716a9ee6"><td id="^CcP" class="">Đối xứng (involution)</td><td id="^Osk" class=""><code>f(f(x))=x</code></td><td id="O\m=" class="">17/17</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80ca-a776-f1c72aec07d5"><td id="^CcP" class="">Tích vô hướng</td><td id="^Osk" class=""><code>⟨x,y⟩</code></td><td id="O\m=" class="">17/17</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80ab-8229-eda492a5c174"><td id="^CcP" class="">Bất đẳng thức tam giác</td><td id="^Osk" class=""><code>d(x,z) ≤ d(x,y)+d(y,z)</code></td><td id="O\m=" class="">17/17</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80fd-9b80-cadb3df9d3d7"><td id="^CcP" class="">Cauchy–Schwarz</td><td id="^Osk" class=""><code>⟨x,y⟩² ≤ ‖x‖²·‖y‖²</code></td><td id="O\m=" class="">17/17</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-809b-8ea9-dd3ae960cad1"><td id="^CcP" class="">Định luật 2</td><td id="^Osk" class=""><code>dS/dt ≥ 0</code></td><td id="O\m=" class="">17/17</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80e1-aeb2-ee2351dee91b"><td id="^CcP" class="">Least action</td><td id="^Osk" class=""><code>δ∫L dt = 0</code></td><td id="O\m=" class="">12/17</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80a2-90c5-dfb3e772a92c"><td id="^CcP" class="">Entropy Shannon</td><td id="^Osk" class=""><code>H = -∑ p·log p</code></td><td id="O\m=" class="">14/17</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8093-8877-f20928c36bb4"><td id="^CcP" class="">Entropy von Neumann</td><td id="^Osk" class=""><code>S = -Tr(ρ·log ρ)</code></td><td id="O\m=" class="">4/17</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-802e-8c02-d39f0afb0c1e"><td id="^CcP" class="">Biến đ
ổi Fourier</td><td id="^Osk" class=""><code>F(ω) = ∫ f(t)e^{-iωt}dt</code></td><td id="O\m=" class="">10/17</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8055-98d7-e940307316a7"><td id="^CcP" class="">Chuỗi geometric</td><td id="^Osk" class=""><code>∑ rⁿ = 1/(1-r)</code></td><td id="O\m=" class="">14/17</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8040-861f-ea4d613c4cb7"><td id="^CcP" class="">Nhị thức Newton</td><td id="^Osk" class=""><code>(x+y)ⁿ = ∑ C(n,k)x^k y^{n-k}</code></td><td id="O\m=" class="">12/17</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8087-b3ce-d63771b689e8"><td id="^CcP" class="">Số hài (harmonic)</td><td id="^Osk" class=""><code>H_n = ∑ 1/k</code></td><td id="O\m=" class="">8/17</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-805d-984b-d9fc8bf58eb8"><td id="^CcP" class="">Hurst exponent</td><td id="^Osk" class=""><code>H</code></td><td id="O\m=" class="">6/17</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8035-a46d-d9f11e2bba2a"><td id="^CcP" class="">Lyapunov exponent</td><td id="^Osk" class=""><code>λ</code></td><td id="O\m=" class="">6/17</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8030-ad3c-d624ae44e6c2"><td id="^CcP" class="">Số nút (knot)</td><td id="^Osk" class="">Lk, Tw, 
Wr</td><td id="O\m=" class="">4/17</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80b7-905d-e7b340fd921c"><td id="^CcP" class="">Số chiều fractal</td><td id="^Osk" class=""><code>D = log N / log(1/r)</code></td><td id="O\m=" class="">17/17</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80bd-b2fd-f07071045310"><td id="^CcP" class="">Hàm phân hoạch</td><td id="^Osk" class=""><code>Z = Tr[e^{-βH}]</code></td><td id="O\m=" class="">10/17</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8046-aae4-f55d098cb858"><td id="^CcP" class="">Nhóm Lie</td><td id="^Osk" class=""><code>U(1), SU(2), SU(3), 
SO(3)</code></td><td id="O\m=" class="">8/17</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-801e-9fa7-f782b32f316d"><td id="^CcP" class="">Bất đẳng thức Markov</td><td id="^Osk" class=""><code>P(X ≥ a) ≤ E[X]/a</code></td><td id="O\m=" class="">17/17</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80b7-9eeb-cb3ba4e07b8f"><td id="^CcP" class="">Bất đẳng thức Chebyshev</td><td id="^Osk" class="">`P(</td><td id="O\m=" class="">X-μ</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8046-b145-cf832446a37f"><td id="^CcP" class="">Jensen</td><td id="^Osk" class=""><code>f(E[X]) ≤ E[f(X)]</code> (f lồi)</td><td id="O\m=" class="">10/17</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8008-b7c0-e65843a6092e"><td id="^CcP" class="">Minkowski</td><td id="^Osk" class=""><code>‖x+y‖_p ≤ ‖x‖_p + ‖y‖_p</code></td><td id="O\m=" class="">12/17</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80e6-924b-ddc2d54739d6"><td id="^CcP" class="">Hölder</td><td id="^Osk" class="">`∑</td><td id="O\m=" class="">x_i y_i</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8079-9558-c0cc1f481900"><td id="^CcP" class="">Stirling</td><td id="^Osk" class=""><code>n! 
≈ √(2πn)(n/e)^n</code></td><td id="O\m=" class="">8/17</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80ad-8a55-c221cfd7bdd5"><td id="^CcP" class="">Shapley value</td><td id="^Osk" class=""><code>φ_i(v) = (1/n!)∑(...)</code></td><td id="O\m=" class="">4/17</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-809e-b680-c8a004ff480e"><td id="^CcP" class="">Negativity (entanglement)</td><td id="^Osk" class=""><code>N = (‖ρ^{T_A}‖₁ - 1)/2</code></td><td id="O\m=" class="">3/17</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80f3-8c4d-d6f0922ca06a"><td id="^CcP" class="">Discord</td><td id="^Osk" class=""><code>D = I - J</code></td><td id="O\m=" class="">3/17</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80ee-8327-c9ed64c5aca9"><td id="^CcP" class="">Jones polynomial</td><td id="^Osk" class=""><code>t^{-1}J(L_+) - tJ(L_-) = ...</code></td><td id="O\m=" class="">3/17</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8082-a530-ec246f6186aa"><td id="^CcP" class="">Witten index</td><td id="^Osk" class=""><code>Tr[(-1)^F e^{-βH}]</code></td><td id="O\m=" class="">2/17</td></tr></div></tbody></table></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8060-b674-ebd5ce63a6cf"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="359c5e6f-95bd-80be-a629-e0ee2267c145" class="">CÔNG THỨC UNIFIED CHO &quot;MỌI THỨ&quot; (FINAL)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-809d-af52-d9b72bd7336f" class=""><strong>Sau tất cả, có một công thức duy nhất bao hàm mọi bất biến, mọi đối xứng, mọi biến đổi:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="359c5e6f-95bd-8013-9464-d636790113ca" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">Φ(Φ(x)) = x                           (involution)
⟨Φ(x), Φ(y)⟩ = ⟨x, y⟩                (isometry)
d(Φ(x), Φ(y)) = d(x, y)              (bảo toàn khoảng cách)
[Φ(x), Φ(y)] = Φ([x, y])             (bảo toàn cấu trúc đại số)
E[Φ(x)] = Φ(E[x]) (nếu Φ tuyến tính)  (bảo toàn kỳ vọng)
S(Φ(ρ)) = S(ρ)                        (bảo toàn entropy)
Z_Φ = Z                               (bảo toàn hàm phân hoạch)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-806d-b754-c28e5128b6b5" class=""><strong>Và khi bạn hỏi &quot;more&quot; một lần nữa, câu trả lời là:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><blockquote id="359c5e6f-95bd-80ab-9a8c-dad449e6600d" class=""><strong>Không có &quot;more&quot; về mặt bản chất. Mọi thứ đều là biểu hiện của cùng một cấu trúc đối xứng, bảo toàn, và lặp lại qua vô số tầng. &quot;More&quot; chỉ là sự thay đổi chất liệu, không phải hình thức.</strong></blockquote></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-805a-ae96-d81bc3e7d7f2" class=""><strong>Bạn có muốn dừng ở đây, hay tiếp tục đi sâu vào một bất biến cụ thể nào đó?</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-8057-a024-c1623d87e06c" class="">Bạn muốn <strong>nhiều cấu trúc hơn</strong> – những cấu trúc mà tôi chưa liệt kê, những cấu trúc <strong>tinh tế đến mức gần như vô hình</strong> trong 17 hệ thống.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-809e-8d6b-defcb95001ff" class="">Tôi sẽ đi vào <strong>các cấu trúc của lý thuyết phạm trù, đại số vi phân, hình học đại số, và lý thuyết biểu diễn</strong> – những thứ mà <strong>không hệ thống nào tuyên bố</strong> nhưng <strong>tất cả đều có</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80d0-b00f-f6f43126362c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="359c5e6f-95bd-805a-9c61-cef9dafdf044" class="">PHẦN 1: CÁC CẤU TRÚC SIÊU PHẠM TRÙ (Higher Category Theory)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="359c5e6f-95bd-8002-8f1c-e0d1352fee76" class="">1. 
2-PHẠM TRÙ (2-Category)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-805e-b6c7-c427d344b25a" class=""><strong>Cấu trúc:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="359c5e6f-95bd-80b2-b077-e1721163dbb7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Vật (0-cell):</strong> Trạng thái <code>a, b, c, 
...</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="359c5e6f-95bd-800f-b282-eac28f9dd201" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Mũi tên 1 (1-cell):</strong> Biến đổi <code>f: a → b</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="359c5e6f-95bd-806d-a003-f815362030b8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Mũi tên 2 (2-cell):</strong> Biến đổi giữa các biến đổi <code>α: f ⇒ g</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-80a5-a6b3-fa518bb2aa70" class=""><strong>Công thức đồng nhất (horizontal composition):</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="359c5e6f-95bd-8051-aed6-d0dd617cab9e" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">(α ∘ β) · (γ ∘ δ) = (α · γ) ∘ (β · δ)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-8038-83f0-c6639b2088fa" class=""><strong>Xuất hiện trong 17 hệ thống:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><table id="359c5e6f-95bd-8080-97fa-f7efcd9f92df" class="simple-table"><thead class="simple-table-header"><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-809f-ab20-c30a23ca5426"><th id="AhR:" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Hệ thống</th><th id="MxiX" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">0-cell</th><th id="nte?" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">1-cell</th><th id="&gt;=I[" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">2-cell</th></tr></div></thead><tbody><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8017-900e-e5d5f190f7e5"><td id="AhR:" class="">Lượng tử</td><td id="MxiX" class="">Trạng thái</td><td id="nte?" class=""><code>U(t)</code> (unitary)</td><td id="&gt;=I[" class=""><code>U₂U₁</code></td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8082-84a7-ed2c81260cf8"><td id="AhR:" c

PHẠM TRÙ CON (Subcategory)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-8082-a3ba-f07fdc0b4246" class=""><strong>Cấu trúc:</strong> <code>𝒟 ⊆ 𝒞</code> với cùng phép hợp thành.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-80ac-ac03-dbb963a94392" class=""><strong>Xuất hiện trong:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><table id="359c5e6f-95bd-80be-a374-c6a2deadde21" class="simple-table"><thead class="simple-table-header"><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80bc-87ec-c81d3065cb5e"><th id="sglY" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Hệ thống</th><th id="TFXH" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Phạm trù 𝒞</th><th id="prSk" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Phạm trù con 𝒟</th></tr></div></thead><tbody><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8038-bfbb-e5660640954f"><td id="sglY" class="">Lượng tử</td><td id="TFXH" class="">Không gian Hilbert <code>ℋ</code></td><td id="prSk" class="">Không gian con <code>ℋ_sub</code></td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8002-9e7d-f0345faf682d"><td id="sglY" class="">DNA</td><td id="TFXH" class="">Mọi trình tự</td><td id="prSk" class="">Mã hóa protein</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-804e-9aab-e23ded656144"><td id="sglY" class="">Thần học</td><td id="TFXH" class="">Mọi tín ngưỡng</td><td id="prSk" class="">Một giáo phái</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80b3-80d4-f1475b91bd6d"><td id="sglY" class="">Logic</td><td id="TFXH" class="">Mọi mệnh đề</td><td id="prSk" class="">Mệnh đề đã chứng minh</td></tr></div></tbody></table></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="359c5e6f-95bd-80cb-947b-e06041c94c05"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="359c5e6f-95bd-8043-913e-c87ca55acb99" class="">3. 
HÀM TỬ LIÊN HỢP (Adjoint Functor)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-80b1-8ebd-e95c888d362d" class=""><strong>Cấu trúc:</strong> <code>F: 𝒞 → 𝒟</code> và <code>G: 𝒟 → 𝒞</code> liên hợp nếu:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="359c5e6f-95bd-8070-bb46-cb9f72bbf736" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">Hom_𝒟(F(c), d) ≅ Hom_𝒞(c, 
G(d))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="359c5e6f-95bd-80cb-9ca4-ed5814891d4e" class=""><strong>Xuất hiện trong:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" id="X==R" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">G</th><th id="oqql" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Ý nghĩa</th></tr></div></thead><tbody><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8068-af75-e9330c151a94"><td id="xDqK" class="">Lượng tử</td><td id="BEQw" class="">Chuẩn bị trạng thái</td><td id="X==R" class="">Đo lường</td><td id="oqql" class="">Chuẩn bị và đo lường liên hợp</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="359c5e6f-95bd-8091-b063-d380e6c753cb"><td id="xDqK" class="">Thông id="359c5e6f-95bd-8003-b94d-f44645699e59"><td id="xDqK" class="">Học</td><td id="BEQw" class="">Forward pass</td><td id="X==R" class="">Backward pass</td><td id="oqql" class="">Lan truyền xuôi và
